openapi: 3.0.3
info:
  title: travelmate-backend API
  version: 1.0.0
  description: Documentation of API endpoints of travelmate-backend
paths:
  /api/about-us/:
    get:
      operationId: about_us_list
      description: |-
        Get About Us content.

        Retrieve the most recent About Us content. Read-only endpoint for regular users.
      parameters:
      - name: page
        required: false
        in: query
        description: A page number within the paginated result set.
        schema:
          type: integer
      tags:
      - about-us
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      - {}
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/PaginatedAboutUsList'
          description: ''
  /api/about-us/{id}/:
    get:
      operationId: about_us_retrieve
      description: |-

          type: integer

            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AboutUs'
          description: ''

  /api/admin/chats/:
    get:
      operationId: admin_chats_list
      description: Admin access to list all support chat sessions. Any admin can view
        and respond to any session.
      summary: 'Admin: List all chat sessions'
      parameters:
      - in: query
        name: created_after
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Filter chats created after this date (supports yyyy-mm-dd, yyyy-mm-ddTHH:MM:SS,
          dd-mm-yyyy, dd-mm-yyyy HH:MM:SS)
      - in: query
        name: created_before
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Filter chats created before this date (supports yyyy-mm-dd, yyyy-mm-ddTHH:MM:SS,
          dd-mm-yyyy, dd-mm-yyyy HH:MM:SS)
      - name: ordering
        required: false
        in: query
        description: Which field to use when ordering the results.
        schema:
          type: string
      - name: page
        required: false
        in: query
        description: A page number within the paginated result set.
        schema:
          type: integer
      - name: search
        required: false
        in: query
        description: A search term.
        schema:
          type: string
      - in: query
        name: status
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Filter chats by status (OPEN, CLOSED, WAITING, ACTIVE)
      - in: query
        name: updated_after
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Filter chats updated after this date (supports yyyy-mm-dd, yyyy-mm-ddTHH:MM:SS,
          dd-mm-yyyy, dd-mm-yyyy HH:MM:SS)
      - in: query
        name: updated_before
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Filter chats updated before this date (supports yyyy-mm-dd, yyyy-mm-ddTHH:MM:SS,
          dd-mm-yyyy, dd-mm-yyyy HH:MM:SS)
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/PaginatedChatSessionList'
          description: ''
  /api/admin/chats/{id}/:
    get:
      operationId: admin_chats_retrieve
      description: Admin access to get a specific chat session with all messages and
        attachments
      summary: 'Admin: Get a specific chat session'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionDetail'
          description: ''
    put:
      operationId: admin_chats_update
      description: Admin access to update a chat session status
      summary: 'Admin: Update a chat session'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSession'
          description: ''
    patch:
      operationId: admin_chats_partial_update
      description: Admin access to partially update a chat session status
      summary: 'Admin: Partially update a chat session'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/PatchedChatSessionRequest'
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/PatchedChatSessionRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/PatchedChatSessionRequest'
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSession'
          description: ''
  /api/admin/chats/{id}/claim/:
    post:
      operationId: admin_chats_claim_create
      description: Allows any admin to claim (take over) an ACTIVE chat. The chat
        will be reassigned to the claiming admin.
      summary: 'Admin: Claim chat session'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSession'
          description: ''
        '400':
          description: Chat is not claimable
  /api/admin/chats/{id}/close/:
    post:
      operationId: admin_chats_close_create
      description: Close a chat session. This prevents further messages until reopened.
      summary: 'Admin: Close chat session'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'

            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSession'
          description: ''
  /api/admin/chats/{id}/delete_session/:
    delete:
      operationId: admin_chats_delete_session_destroy
      description: Admin endpoint to delete a chat session and all its messages
      summary: 'Admin: Delete chat session'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '204':
          description: Chat session successfully deleted
        '404':
          description: Chat session not found
        '403':
          description: Not authorized to delete this chat session
  /api/admin/chats/{id}/export_pdf/:
    get:
      operationId: admin_chats_export_pdf_retrieve
      description: Generate and download a PDF document containing the chat session
        details and messages
      summary: 'Admin: Export chat session as PDF'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: string
                format: binary
          description: PDF file containing chat session details
  /api/admin/chats/{id}/mark_as_read/:
    post:
      operationId: admin_chats_mark_as_read_create
      description: Marks all user messages in the session as read
      summary: 'Admin: Mark messages as read'
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
      tags:
      - Chat Admin API
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'

            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatSessionRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  status:
                    type: string
          description: ''

    post:
      operationId: admin_invitations_accept_create
      description: "\n    Accepts an admin invitation.\n    You must provide the invited\
        \ email, token, and a valid password.\n\n    * Accessible by any invited user.\n\
        \    "
      summary: Accept Invitation
      tags:
      - Admin Features
      - Invitation Handling
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/AcceptInvitationRequest'
            examples:
              AcceptInvitationRequest:
                value:
                  email: user@example.com
                  token: your-token-here
                  password: NewSecurePassword123!
                summary: Accept Invitation Request
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/AcceptInvitationRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/AcceptInvitationRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - {}
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AcceptInvitationResponse'
              examples:
                SuccessResponse:
                  value:
                    message: Invitation accepted, password set, and role assigned.
                    error: false
                  summary: Success Response
          description: Invitation accepted and user activated.
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AcceptInvitationResponse'
              examples:
                MissingFields:
                  value:
                    message: Email, token, and password are required.
                    error: true
                  summary: Missing Fields
          description: Missing/invalid data or token.
        '404':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AcceptInvitationResponse'
              examples:
                InvitationNotFound:
                  value:
                    message: Invitation not found or already accepted.
                    error: true
                  summary: Invitation Not Found
          description: Invitation not found or already accepted.

  /api/admin/reports/bookings/breakdown/:
    get:
      operationId: admin_reports_bookings_breakdown_list
      description: "\n    Return a list of buckets, each with\n    - label (e.g. Jan\
        \ 2025),\n    - flight_bookings\n    - car_bookings\n    - stay_bookings\n\
        \    - (flight_revenue, car_revenue, stay_revenue if superuser).\n\n    You\
        \ may filter by:\n    1. An explicit (start=YYYY-MM-DD&end=YYYY-MM-DD) plus\
        \ optional group_by={day,week,month};\n    2. A calendar period={day,week,month,year};\n\
        \    3. The last N months (months=1–12, default 12).\n\n    If you are a regular\
        \ admin, the revenue fields are omitted.\n\n    *Accessible by all admins.\n\
        \    "
      summary: Get individual booking stats
      parameters:
      - in: query
        name: end
        schema:
          type: string
        description: End date (`YYYY-MM-DD`). Must be used with `start`.
        examples:
          June302025:
            value: '2025-06-30'
            summary: June 30 2025
      - in: query
        name: group_by
        schema:
          type: string
        description: 'When providing `start` & `end`, how to bucket: `day`, `week`,
          or `month`. `day` → bucket per date; `week` → bucket per Mon→Sun; `month`
          → bucket per calendar month.'
        examples:
          Daily:
            value: day
          Weekly:
            value: week
          Monthly:
            value: month
      - in: query
        name: months
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: Number of past calendar months (1–12) to include. Defaults to
          12. Ignored if `period` or `start`/`end` provided.
        examples:
          Last6Months:
            value: 6
            summary: Last 6 months
          Last12Months:
            value: 12
            summary: Last 12 months
      - name: page
        required: false
        in: query
        description: A page number within the paginated result set.
        schema:
          type: integer
      - in: query
        name: period
        schema:
          type: string
        description: One of `day`, `week`, `month`, or `year`. Overrides `months`.
          `day` → today; `week` → this week; `month` → this month; `year` → this year.
        examples:
          ThisWeek:
            value: week
            summary: This week
          ThisMonth:
            value: month
            summary: This month
          ThisYear:
            value: year
            summary: This year
      - in: query
        name: start
        schema:
          type: string

  /api/admin/reports/bookings/combined/:
    get:
      operationId: admin_reports_bookings_combined_list
      description: "\n    Return a list of { label, bookings, revenue? } buckets—either\
        \ by:\n\n    1. Explicit range (start=YYYY-MM-DD&end=YYYY-MM-DD) with an optional\
        \ group_by={day,week,month};\n    2. Calendar period (period={day,week,month,year});\n\
        \    3. Last N months (months=1–12, default 6).\n\n    Priority:\n    1. If\
        \ both start and end are provided, use that exact range.\n    2. Else if period\
        \ is provided, compute the calendar interval (e.g. Monday→today for week).\n\
        \    3. Otherwise use months=X (1–12) or default to 6, producing one bucket\
        \ per calendar month.\n\n    - When grouping by day, you’ll receive one bucket\
        \ per date (label=\"YYYY-MM-DD\").\n    - When grouping by week, you’ll receive\
        \ one bucket per week (label=\"Week of YYYY-MM-DD\").\n    - When grouping\
        \ by month, you’ll receive one bucket per month (label=\"Jun 2025\").\n\n\
        \    Each bucket always has:\n    - label: the day/week/month string.\n  \
        \  - bookings: sum of flight + car + stay bookings in that bucket.\n    -\
        \ revenue: ONLY if superuser, sum of flight + car + stay revenue.\n\n    If\
        \ you are a regular admin, the revenue field is omitted from every bucket.\n\
        \n    *Accessible by all admins.\n    "
      summary: Get combined booking stats
      parameters:
      - in: query
        name: end
        schema:
          type: string
        description: End date (`YYYY-MM-DD`) for explicit range; must be used with
          `start`.
        examples:
          June302025:
            value: '2025-06-30'
            summary: June 30 2025
      - in: query
        name: group_by
        schema:
          type: string
        description: 'When `start` & `end` are provided, how to bucket: `day`, `week`,
          or `month`. `day` → one bucket per date; `week` → one bucket per Mon→Sun;
          `month` → one bucket per calendar month.'
        examples:
          Daily:
            value: day
          Weekly:
            value: week
          Monthly:
            value: month
      - in: query
        name: months
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: Number of past calendar months (1–12) to include. Defaults to
          6. Ignored if `period` or `start`/`end` present.
        examples:
          Last3Months:
            value: 3
            summary: Last 3 months
          Last6Months:
            value: 6
            summary: Last 6 months
          Last12Months:
            value: 12
            summary: Last 12 months
      - name: page
        required: false
        in: query
        description: A page number within the paginated result set.
        schema:
          type: integer
      - in: query
        name: period
        schema:
          type: string
        description: One of `day`, `week`, `month`, or `year`. Overrides `months`.
          `day` → only today; `week` → Monday→today; `month` → first of month→today;
          `year` → Jan 1→today.
        examples:
          ThisWeek:
            value: week
            summary: This week
          ThisMonth:
            value: month
            summary: This month
          ThisYear:
            value: year
            summary: This year
      - in: query
        name: start
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Start date (`YYYY-MM-DD`) for explicit range; must be used with
          `end`. Overrides `period`/`months`.
        examples:
          June12025:
            value: '2025-06-01'
            summary: June 1 2025
      tags:
      - Admin Features
      - Reports & Analytics
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:

  /api/admin/reports/summary/:
    get:
      operationId: admin_reports_summary_retrieve
      description: "\n    Return “total_users”, “total_bookings” (and, if superuser,\
        \ “total_revenue”) plus their growth percentages—over any of:\n\n    1. An\
        \ explicit date range (start=YYYY-MM-DD&end=YYYY-MM-DD),\n    2. A calendar\
        \ “period” (period={day,week,month,year}), or\n    3. The last N calendar\
        \ months (months), defaulting to 6.\n\n    Priority:\n    1. If both start\
        \ and end are provided, they override all other parameters.\n    2. Else if\
        \ period is provided, compute from today’s calendar boundaries.\n    3. Otherwise\
        \ use months=X (1–12) or fallback to 6.\n\n    - start & end (both YYYY-MM-DD)\
        \ returns metrics for every day in that range (aggregated across the entire\
        \ interval—no per-day breakdown here).\n    - period = day  → only today’s\
        \ metrics.\n    - period = week → Monday of this week through today.\n   \
        \ - period = month → first of this month through today.\n    - period = year\
        \ → Jan 1 of this year through today.\n\n    - months = integer (1–12) → aggregate\
        \ over the last N calendar months, one bucket per month, then summed into\
        \ a single top‐line summary.\n\n    If you are a regular admin, the two revenue\
        \ fields (\"total_revenue\" and \"revenue_growth_percentage\") are omitted.\n\
        \n    *Accessible by all admins.\n    "
      summary: Get dashboard summary data
      parameters:
      - in: query
        name: end
        schema:
          type: string
        description: End date (`YYYY-MM-DD`). Must be used together with `start`.
        examples:
          June302025:
            value: '2025-06-30'
            summary: June 30 2025
      - in: query
        name: months
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: Number of past calendar months to include (1–12). Defaults to
          6. Ignored if `period` or both `start` and `end` are present.
        examples:
          Last3Months:
            value: 3
            summary: Last 3 months
          Last6Months:
            value: 6
            summary: Last 6 months
          Last12Months:
            value: 12
            summary: Last 12 months
      - in: query
        name: period
        schema:
          type: string
        description: One of `day`, `week`, `month`, or `year`. Overrides `months`
          if present. `day` → only today; `week` → Monday through today; `month` →
          first of this month through today; `year` → Jan 1 through today.
        examples:
          Today:
            value: day
          ThisWeek:
            value: week
            summary: This week
          ThisMonth:
            value: month
            summary: This month
          ThisYear:
            value: year
            summary: This year
      - in: query
        name: start
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Start date (`YYYY-MM-DD`). Must be used together with `end`.
          Takes precedence over `period`/`months`.
        examples:
          June12025:
            value: '2025-06-01'
            summary: June 1 2025

  /api/auth/social/facebook/:
    post:
      operationId: auth_social_facebook_create
      description: "\n        Authenticate users using Facebook OAuth.\n\n       \
        \ **How it Works:**\n        1. The user selects \"Sign in with Facebook\"\
        \ in the frontend application.\n        2. Facebook provides an access token\
        \ upon successful authentication.\n        3. The frontend sends this access\
        \ token to this endpoint.\n        4. The API verifies the token with Facebook\
        \ and, if valid:\n            - Creates a user account (if they are new).\n\
        \            - Returns a Django authentication token for further API requests.\n\
        \n        **Request Format:**\n        Send a `POST` request with a valid\
        \ `access_token` obtained from Facebook.\n\n        **Example Request:**\n\
        \        ```json\n        {\n            \"access_token\": \"EAAJZ...\"\n\
        \        }\n        ```\n\n        **Response Format:**\n        If authentication\
        \ is successful, the API returns an authentication token.\n\n        **Example\
        \ Response:**\n        ```json\n        {\n            \"key\": \"7f4e265c8f8c5e64db...\"\
        \n        }\n        ```\n        "
      summary: Facebook OAuth Login
      tags:
      - auth
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                access_token:
                  type: string
                  description: Facebook OAuth access token obtained after successful
                    login with Facebook.
              required:
              - access_token
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      - {}
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  key:
                    type: string
                    description: Django authentication token to be used in future
                      requests.
          description: ''
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: Invalid access token or authentication failed.
          description: ''
  /api/auth/social/google/:
    post:
      operationId: auth_social_google_create
      description: "\n        Authenticate users using Google OAuth and return JWT\
        \ tokens.\n\n        **Flow:**\n        1. User signs in with Google on the\
        \ frontend\n        2. Frontend receives Google access token\n        3. Frontend\
        \ sends token to this endpoint\n        4. Backend verifies token with Google\n\
        \        5. If valid, returns JWT tokens for API authentication\n\n      \
        \  **Note:** The Google access token should be obtained through official Google\
        \ OAuth flow.\n        "
      summary: Google OAuth Login (JWT)
      tags:
      - Authentication
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                access_token:
                  type: string
                  description: Access token from Google OAuth flow
                  example: ya29.a0AfH6SM...
              required:
              - access_token
            examples:
              ExampleRequest:
                value:
                  access_token: ya29.a0AfH6SM...
                summary: Example Request
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      - {}
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  refresh:
                    type: string
                    description: JWT refresh token (long-lived)
                  access:
                    type: string
                    description: JWT access token (short-lived)
              examples:
                SuccessResponse:
                  value:
                    refresh: eyJhbGciOiJIUz...
                    access: eyJhbGciOiJIUz...
                  summary: Success Response
          description: ''
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: Invalid Google token or authentication failed
                examples:
                  application/json:
                    error: Invalid Google access token
          description: ''
        '401':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: Authentication failed
                examples:
                  application/json:
                    detail: Authentication credentials were not provided.
          description: ''

        \ (optional): ongoing | completed | cancelled\n    - `payment_status` (optional):\
        \ paid | pending | failed | refunded\n    - `from_date`, `to_date` (optional,\
        \ YYYY-MM-DD)\n    "
      summary: List all bookings with filtering

            summary: Start of year
          LastMonth:
            value: '2024-11-01'
            summary: Last month
      - in: query
        name: payment_status
        schema:
          type: string
          enum:
          - failed

          - refunded

          CurrentDate:

            summary: Current date

      - Admin Bookings

  /api/chat/messages/:
    post:
      operationId: chat_messages_create
      description: "Sends a new message in a chat session with optional file attachment.\n\
        \n        For users:\n        - Can only send messages to their own sessions\n\
        \        - If session is closed, it will be reopened in WAITING status\n\n\
        \        For admins:\n        - Can send messages to any session\n       \
        \ - If session is in WAITING status, it will be changed to ACTIVE\n      \
        \  - If session is closed, it will be reopened in ACTIVE status\n\n      \
        \  File attachment:\n        - Single file can be attached to a message\n\
        \        - File is stored in chat_attachments/session_id/message_id/\n   \
        \     - Max file size: 10MB\n        - Allowed file types: PDF, DOC, DOCX,\
        \ JPG, JPEG, PNG\n        "
      summary: Send a chat message
      tags:
      - Chat API
      requestBody:
        content:
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatMessageRequest'
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatMessageRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '201':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatMessage'
          description: ''

  /api/notification-prefrence/:
    get:
      operationId: notification_prefrence_list
      description: |-
        Returns the authenticated user's notification preferences.

        Notification preferences determine **which types of notifications** the user is willing to receive (e.g. info, alerts, booking updates) and **how they are delivered** (in-app, email, push).

        These preferences are evaluated by the backend at the time a notification is dispatched. If a notification type is disabled here, it will not be delivered to the user, regardless of channel.
      summary: List notification preferences
      parameters:
      - name: page
        required: false
        in: query
        description: A page number within the paginated result set.
        schema:
          type: integer
      - name: page_size
        required: false
        in: query
        description: Number of results to return per page.
        schema:
          type: integer
      tags:
      - Notification Preferences
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/PaginatedNotificationPreferenceList'
          description: ''
    post:
      operationId: notification_prefrence_create
      description: |-
        Create a notification preference profile for the authenticated user.

        This endpoint allows the client to define:
        - Which **notification types** the user wants to receive
        - Which **delivery channels** should be used

        If preferences are not explicitly created, the system will automatically generate default preferences when the first notification is sent.
      summary: Create notification preferences
      tags:
      - Notification Preferences
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/NotificationPreferenceRequest'
            examples:
              NotificationPreferencesExample:
                value:
                  enabled_types:
                  - info
                  - warning
                  - error
                  - alert
                  - new_message
                  - ticket_resolved
                  - ticket_escalated
                  - ticket_claimed
                  - booking_update
                  - booking_reminder
                  - schedule_change
                  - marketing_and_promotional
                  - news_and_updates
                  enabled_channels:
                  - db
                  - email
                  - push
                summary: Example preference payload using supported enum values
                description: This example demonstrates how a client can configure
                  which notification TYPES they want to receive and through which
                  DELIVERY CHANNELS. Only values defined in the backend enums are
                  accepted.
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/NotificationPreferenceRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/NotificationPreferenceRequest'
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '201':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/NotificationPreference'
              examples:
                NotificationPreferencesExample:
                  value:
                    enabled_types:
                    - info
                    - warning
                    - error
                    - alert
                    - new_message
                    - ticket_resolved
                    - ticket_escalated
                    - ticket_claimed
                    - booking_update
                    - booking_reminder
                    - schedule_change
                    - marketing_and_promotional
                    - news_and_updates
                    enabled_channels:
                    - db
                    - email
                    - push
                  summary: Example preference payload using supported enum values
                  description: This example demonstrates how a client can configure
                    which notification TYPES they want to receive and through which
                    DELIVERY CHANNELS. Only values defined in the backend enums are
                    accepted.
          description: ''

  /api/notifications/:
    get:
      operationId: notifications_list
      description: |-
        Returns a paginated list of notifications delivered to the authenticated user.

        Each item represents a **NotificationRecipient**, which is a user-specific instance of a system-generated notification. The same notification event may appear in multiple users' inboxes.

        The response includes metadata such as read status, timestamps, notification type, category, and optional action links.
      summary: List notifications (inbox)
      parameters:
      - in: query
        name: is_read
        schema:
          type: boolean
        description: Filter notifications by read status.
      - in: query
        name: ordering
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Order notifications by `created_at` or `read_at`. Prefix with
          `-` for descending order.
      - name: page
        required: false
        in: query
        description: A page number within the paginated result set.
        schema:
          type: integer
      - in: query
        name: search
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Search notifications by title or message content. Useful for
          client-side filtering and search experiences.
      tags:
      - Notifications
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/PaginatedNotificationRecipientList'
          description: ''

  /api/notifications/bulk-delete/:
    post:
      operationId: notifications_bulk_delete_create
      description: |-
        Deletes multiple notifications from the authenticated user's inbox.

        Important:
        - `notification_ids` must contain **NotificationRecipient IDs**, not Notification IDs.
        - Each NotificationRecipient represents a user-specific instance of a notification.
        - This operation performs a **soft delete** by setting `visible = false`.
        - Soft-deleted notifications will no longer appear in list or retrieve responses.
        - The underlying notification event remains intact for other recipients.

        This operation is executed atomically.
      summary: Delete multiple notifications
      tags:
      - Notifications
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/BulkDeleteRequest'
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/BulkDeleteRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/BulkDeleteRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          description: |-
            Notifications successfully deleted.

            The response includes the number of notifications that were removed from the authenticated user's inbox.
        '400':
          description: Validation error. One or more notification IDs are invalid,
            do not belong to the authenticated user, or refer to already deleted notifications.
        '401':
          description: Authentication credentials were not provided or are invalid.
  /api/notifications/bulk-mark-read/:
    post:
      operationId: notifications_bulk_mark_read_create
      description: |-
        Marks multiple notifications as read for the authenticated user.

        Important:
        - `notification_ids` must contain **NotificationRecipient IDs**, not Notification IDs.
        - Each NotificationRecipient represents a user-specific instance of a notification.
        - This operation only affects the authenticated user's notifications.
        - Other recipients of the same notification are not affected.

        This endpoint performs a bulk update and is executed atomically.
      summary: Mark multiple notifications as read
      tags:
      - Notifications
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/BulkMarkAsReadRequest'
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/BulkMarkAsReadRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/BulkMarkAsReadRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          description: |-
            Notifications successfully marked as read.

            The response includes the number of notifications that were updated.
        '400':
          description: Validation error. One or more notification IDs are invalid,
            do not belong to the authenticated user, or refer to deleted notifications.
        '401':
          description: Authentication credentials were not provided or are invalid.

          description: ''

                  error: true
          description: ''

          content:
            application/json:

  /api/superadmin/roles/{id}/cancel-invite/:
    post:
      operationId: superadmin_roles_cancel_invite_create
      description: "\n    Revoke an outstanding invitation for a given email on the\
        \ specified role.\n    Only pending invitations (not yet accepted) can be\
        \ revoked.\n\n    * Accessible only by superadmins.\n    "
      summary: Cancel (revoke) a pending role invitation - Admin
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: A unique integer value identifying this Role.
        required: true
      tags:
      - Superadmin
      - Roles Management
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                email:
                  type: string
                  format: email
                  description: Email address of the invited user to cancel
              required:
              - email
              example:
                email: invitee@example.com
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '204':
          description: Invitation successfully canceled; no content returned
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: Invalid request (e.g. missing email)
                example:
                  message: Email is required.
                  error: true
          description: ''
        '404':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: No pending invitation found for this email and role
                example:
                  message: No pending invitation found for invitee@example.com on
                    this role.
                  error: true
          description: ''
        '500':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: Server error while attempting to cancel invitation
                example:
                  message: Failed to cancel invitation. Please try again later.
                  error: true
          description: ''
  /api/superadmin/roles/{id}/invite/:
    post:
      operationId: superadmin_roles_invite_create
      description: "\n    Invite a new user to the admin dashboard by sending an invitation\
        \ email\n    If the email has already been invited and not accepted, the previous\
        \ invitation will be marked as invalid.\n\n    * Accessible only by superadmins.\n\
        \    "
      summary: Invite admin by email
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: A unique integer value identifying this Role.
        required: true
      tags:
      - Superadmin
      - Roles Management
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/InvitedUserRequest'
            examples:
              InviteAdminRequest:
                value:
                  email: admin.invitee@example.com
                  name: Admin Invitee
                summary: Invite Admin Request
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/InvitedUserRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/InvitedUserRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '201':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/InviteAdminResponse'
              examples:
                SuccessResponse:
                  value:
                    message: Invitation sent successfully.
                    error: false
                  summary: Success Response
          description: Invitation sent successfully.
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
              examples:
                MissingEmailError:
                  value:
                    message: Email is required.
                    error: true
                MissingNameError:
                  value:
                    message: Invitee’s name is required.
                    error: true
          description: Validation error (e.g., missing/invalid email or name).
  /api/superadmin/roles/{id}/remove/:
    post:
      operationId: superadmin_roles_remove_create
      description: "\n    Remove an existing admin from the specified role by their\
        \ email address.\n    If the admin is not part of the role, an error will\
        \ be returned\n\n    * Accessible only by superadmins.\n    "
      summary: Remove an admin from a role
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: A unique integer value identifying this Role.
        required: true
      tags:
      - Superadmin
      - Roles Management
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                email:
                  type: string
                  format: email
                  description: Email address of the admin to remove from the role
              required:
              - email
              example:
                email: admin_to_remove@example.com
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '204':
          description: Admin removed successfully from the role
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: User is not a member of this role or invalid input
                example:
                  message: User user_to_remove@example.com is not part of the role.
                  error: true
          description: ''
        '404':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: User with the provided email does not exist
                example:
                  message: User with email user_to_remove@example.com does not exist.
                  error: true
          description: ''

  /api/superadmin/superadmins/cancel-invite/:
    post:
      operationId: superadmin_superadmins_cancel_invite_create
      description: "\n    Cancel an outstanding superadmin invitation by email.\n\
        \    Only invitations that have *not* yet been accepted can be revoked.\n\n\
        \    * Accessible only by superadmins.\n    "
      summary: ' Cancel (Revoke) a pending superadmin invitation'
      tags:
      - Superadmin
      - Superadmin Features
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                email:
                  type: string
                  format: email
                  description: Email address of the invited superadmin to revoke
              required:
              - email
              example:
                email: new.superadmin@example.com
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '204':
          description: Invitation successfully canceled; no content returned
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: Invalid request (e.g. missing email)
                example:
                  message: Email is required.
                  error: true
          description: ''
        '404':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: No pending superadmin invitation found for this email.
                example:
                  message: No pending superadmin invitation found for new.superadmin@example.com.
                  error: true
          description: ''
        '500':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                description: Server error while attempting to cancel invitation
                example:
                  message: Failed to cancel invitation. Please try again later.
                  error: true
          description: ''
  /api/superadmin/superadmins/invite/:
    post:
      operationId: superadmin_superadmins_invite_create
      description: "\n    Transfer your superadmin privileges to a new user by sending\
        \ them an invitation.\n\n    You must also decide what happens to your own\
        \ access:\n    - \"change_role\" assigns you a new role (must include \"new_role_id\"\
        )\n    - \"remove_access\" removes your admin privileges entirely\n\n    *\
        \ Only current superadmins can perform this action\n    "
      summary: Invite a new user to become a superadmin
      tags:
      - Superadmin
      - Superadmin Features
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/InviteSuperadminRequest'
            examples:
              InviteRequest:
                value:
                  email: new.superadmin@example.com
                  name: New Superadmin
                  transfer_action: change_role
                  new_role_id: 3
                summary: Invite Request
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/InviteSuperadminRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/InviteSuperadminRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '201':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/InviteAdminResponse'
              examples:
                201Success:
                  value:
                    message: Invitation sent successfully.
                    error: false
                  summary: 201 Success
          description: Invitation sent successfully.
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
              examples:
                400MissingEmail:
                  value:
                    message: Target user email is required.
                    error: true
                  summary: 400 Missing Email
          description: Validation error.
        '409':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
              examples:
                409Duplicate:
                  value:
                    message: A user with this email already exists.
                    error: true
                  summary: 409 Duplicate
          description: Duplicate invitation.
        '500':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
              examples:
                500Unexpected:
                  value:
                    message: An unexpected error occurred.
                    error: true
                    cause: …
                  summary: 500 Unexpected
          description: Server error.
  /api/superadmin/superadmins/transfer/:
    post:
      operationId: transfer_superadmin_privilege
      description: "\n    Promote an admin to superadmin and adjust your own access.\n\
        \n    - Downgrade to another role (using \"change_role\" action and providing\
        \ \"new_role_id\"),\n    - Or completely remove your own access (\"remove_access\"\
        \ action).\n\n    * Only current superadmins can perform this action\n    "
      summary: Transfer Superadmin Privilege
      tags:
      - Superadmin
      - Superadmin Features
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/SuperadminTransferRequest'
            examples:
              RequestExample:
                value:
                  email: new.superadmin@example.com
                  transfer_action: change_role
                  new_role_id: 3
                summary: Request Example
          application/x-www-form-urlencoded:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/SuperadminTransferRequest'
          multipart/form-data:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/SuperadminTransferRequest'
        required: true
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/TransferResponse'
              examples:
                200Success:
                  value:
                    message: Privileges transferred.
                    error: false
                  summary: 200 Success
          description: Transfer successful.
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/TransferResponse'
              examples:
                400MissingEmail:
                  value:
                    message: Target admin email is required.
                    error: true
                  summary: 400 Missing Email
                400InvalidAction:
                  value:
                    message: Invalid transfer_action.
                    error: true
                  summary: 400 Invalid Action
                400MissingRoleID:
                  value:
                    message: new_role_id is required for change_role.
                    error: true
                  summary: 400 Missing Role ID
          description: Invalid input or conditions.
        '404':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/TransferResponse'
              examples:
                404NotFound:
                  value:
                    message: Admin with this email does not exist.
                    error: true
                  summary: 404 Not Found
          description: Admin not found.

  /api/superuser/{id}/activate/:
    patch:
      operationId: admin_activate_user
      description: |-
        Activate a user account by setting the `is_active` field to `True`.

        The request must include a reason for activation, either from a predefined list (`reason_choices`) or a custom explanation (`additional_reason`).

        Upon successful activation, the user is re-enabled in the system and the reason is logged for auditing purposes.
      summary: Activate a User Account
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: A unique integer value identifying this user.
        required: true
      tags:
      - Super Admin Management
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                reason_choices:
                  type: string
                  example: Reinstated by admin
                  description: Optional predefined reason for activating the user.
                additional_reason:
                  type: string
                  example: User submitted an appeal and provided necessary documents.
                  description: Optional custom reason for activating the user.
              required: []
            examples:
              SuccessfulActivation:
                value:
                  reason_choices: Manual reactivation by admin
                  additional_reason: Issue with suspension was resolved
                summary: Successful Activation
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  status:
                    type: integer
                    example: 200
                  message:
                    type: string
                    example: User has been activated
                  error:
                    type: boolean
                    example: false
              examples:
                ActivationFailure-AlreadyActive:
                  value:
                    status: 400
                    message: User is already active
                    error: true
                  summary: Activation Failure - Already Active
          description: User account was successfully activated.
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  status:
                    type: integer
                    example: 400
                  message:
                    type: string
                    example: User is already active
                  error:
                    type: boolean
                    example: true
          description: User is already active or request data is invalid.
  /api/superuser/{id}/deactivate/:
    patch:
      operationId: admin_deactivate_user
      description: |-
        Deactivate a user account by setting the `is_active` field to `False`.

        The request must include a reason for deactivation, either selected from a predefined list (`reason_choices`) or provided as a custom explanation (`additional_reason`).

        Upon successful deactivation, an email is automatically sent to the user notifying them of the reason.
      summary: Deactivate a User Account
      parameters:
      - in: path
        name: id
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: A unique integer value identifying this user.
        required: true
      tags:
      - Super Admin Management
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                reason_choices:
                  type: string
                  example: Violation of terms
                  description: Optional predefined reason for deactivating the user.
                additional_reason:
                  type: string
                  example: User breached platform rules multiple times.
                  description: Optional custom reason for deactivating the user.
              required: []
            examples:
              SuccessfulDeactivation:
                value:
                  reason_choices: Violation of terms
                  additional_reason: User repeatedly violated posting guidelines
                summary: Successful Deactivation
      security:
      - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  status:
                    type: integer
                    example: 200
                  message:
                    type: string
                    example: User has been deactivated
                  error:
                    type: boolean
                    example: false
              examples:
                DeactivationFailure-AlreadyInactive:
                  value:
                    status: 400
                    message: User is already deactivated
                    error: true
                  summary: Deactivation Failure - Already Inactive
          description: User account was successfully deactivated.
        '400':
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  status:
                    type: integer
                    example: 400
                  message:
                    type: string
                    example: User is already deactivated
                  error:
                    type: boolean
                    example: true
          description: User is already deactivated or request data is invalid.

  /api/transfers/search-terminal-to-gps/:
    get:
      operationId: transfers_search_terminal_to_gps_retrieve
      description: "\n    Search for transfers from a terminal (IATA) to GPS coordinates\
        \ using Hotelbeds API with intelligent fallback system.\n\n    ## Intelligent\
        \ Fallback System\n\n    When GPS coordinates return no transfer services,\
        \ the system automatically:\n    1. **Fast Mode (default)**: Quickly maps\
        \ GPS to nearest known locations\n    2. **Extended Mode**: Comprehensive\
        \ search through nearby hotels, terminals, and destinations\n    3. **User\
        \ Guidance**: Provides actionable suggestions when no services are found\n\
        \n    ## Fallback Behavior\n\n    - **Automatic**: Fallback runs by default\
        \ when GPS coordinates fail\n    - **Smart Mapping**: GPS coordinates are\
        \ mapped to nearest Hotelbeds-supported locations\n    - **Transparent**:\
        \ Users see exactly what fallback locations were attempted\n    - **Helpful**:\
        \ Clear guidance on what to try next\n\n    ## Response Structure\n\n    -\
        \ **Success**: Returns transfer services with fallback information\n    -\
        \ **No Services**: Returns attempted fallback locations with user guidance\n\
        \    - **Caching**: Responses are cached for 30 minutes for performance\n\n\
        \    ## Examples\n\n    - **CDG Airport → Eiffel Tower GPS**: Automatically\
        \ maps to nearby hotels\n    - **LOS Airport → Lagos GPS**: Maps to nearest\
        \ supported locations\n    - **Custom Radius**: Adjust search area with radius_km\
        \ parameter\n    "
      summary: Search for transfers from terminal (IATA) to GPS coordinates with intelligent
        fallback
      parameters:
      - in: query
        name: adults
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
        examples:
          Adults:
            value: '2'
      - in: query
        name: children
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
        examples:
          Children:
            value: '0'
      - in: query
        name: departing
        schema:
          type: string
        required: true
        examples:
          DepartingDateTime:
            value: '2025-07-15T18:39:00'
            summary: Departing DateTime
      - in: query
        name: direction
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Filter by direction. Accepts 'ARRIVAL', 'DEPARTURE', or a comma-separated
          list. Case-insensitive.
        examples:
          Direction:
            value: arrival,departure
      - in: query
        name: enable_fallback
        schema:
          type: boolean
        description: 'Enable intelligent fallback when GPS returns no results (default:
          true)'
        examples:
          EnableFallback:
            value: true
            summary: Enable Fallback
      - in: query
        name: fallback_budget_ms
        schema:
          type: integer
        description: 'Timeout budget in milliseconds for fallback search (default:

          - `Security concern`

        - `additional_feedback` is optional for other reasons.

        Upon successful request:
        - The account will be deleted.
        - The user will be logged out.
        - The deletion reason will be recorded for internal analytics.
